```
GLOBAL = {}
def start_opt(n, beta, scale=1e6):
   S=np.sqrt(2*n); S2=S*S
   fsize = 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length()  # but 3*n maybe large. For n=16384, 49150 -> 65536 fsize. okay.
   GLOBAL.update(n=n,S=S,S2=S2,fsize=fsize,beta=float(beta),scale=float(scale), best_c=np.inf, best_p=None, count=0)

def fun(u):
   state=GLOBAL
   u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64)
   # center mean to keep stable (affine)
   u = u - np.mean(u)
   # softmax
   m=np.max(u)
   # if m is huge? u centered, max maybe large. exp(u-m) safe if u-m ≤0. If some underflow 0.
   e=np.exp(u-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <=0: # all underflow? shouldn't
      p=np.full(n,1/n)
   else:
      p=e/s
   # if some p exactly 0, gradient zero; okay. Could add tiny floor for numerical? Maybe not.
   A=fft(p, fsize)
   q=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
   c=S2*q
   # Remove tiny imaginary/negative
   cm=np.max(c)
   bm=state['beta']
   # if bm too high, exp may underflow only negative, okay
   z=bm*(c-cm)
   # To avoid exp(0)=1 fine. If z contains nan? no.
   # If c all equal? ok.
   ez=np.exp(z)
   tot=ez.sum()
   if tot==0:
       val=cm
       w=None
   else:
       w=ez/tot
       val=cm + np.log(tot)/bm
   grad_p = 4? wait if F=val (LSE c) gradient wrt p: 2*S2*Corr(w,p). Since derivative q wrt p_i =2∑w p? Let's derive with c=S^2 q.
   grad_q_i = 2*Corr(w,p)  (w normalized over q/c same weights, derivative LSE c wrt q? If F=LSE(c), gradient wrt c=w, so wrt q=S^2 w. Then wrt p_i = S^2 * ∑ (S^2 w_k)∂q_k/∂p_i = S^4? Wait let's be careful.
     q_k = p*p. c_k = S^2 q_k.
     F = LSEβ(c). ∂F/∂c_k = w_k.
     ∂F/∂q_k = w_k * S^2.
     ∂F/∂p_i = ∑_k (w_k S^2) ∂q_k/∂p_i.
     ∂q_k/∂p_i = 2 p_{k-i} (if valid). So ∂F/∂p_i = 2 S^2 ∑_k w_k p_{k-i} = 2 S^2 Corr(w,p).
   Yes not S^4. Good.
   corr = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
   grad_p = 2*S2*corr
   # If w None? use uniform? But tot not 0.
   grad_u = p * (grad_p - np.dot(p, grad_p))
   # Update best exact
   if cm < state['best_c'] - 1e-14:
       state['best_c']=cm; state['best_p']=p.copy(); state['best_u']=u.copy()? Maybe not.
   state['count'] +=1
   return state['scale']*val, state['scale']*grad_u
```

Let's include a `polish_p` function:
```
def polish_p(p, n, max_time, rng, beta? exact):
   S2=2n; p=normalize positive sum1
   lr=0.05
   for steps:
      A=fft(p,fsize); q=...; c=S2*q; cm=max
      # weights: softmax beta_exact large dynamic: temp=max(1e-6, (cm-c).std())? Use top uniform for near max.
      if cm ==0? break
      top = c >= cm - 1e-5*(1+cm)
      if top.sum()==0: top[max]=1
      w = zeros; w[top]=1/top.sum()
      corr=ifft(W*conj(A)); g=2*S2*corr[:n]
      # project
      p_new = project_simplex(p - lr*g, 1.0)
      # line search: try lr multiples; choose best cm
      best = p,cm
      for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]: test...
      if no improve, lr*=0.7; noise small
   return best
```

Implementation details of `objective_from_x`:
```
def objective_from_x(x, state):
    u = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = state['n']
    fsize=state['fsize']
    S2=state['S2']
    beta=state['beta']
    # center u to avoid large mean (affine)
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    # softmax
    u_max = u.max()
    # If u_max is very negative? exp okay. Use shifted.
    e = np.exp(u - u_max)
    s = e.sum()
    if s == 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       # fallback uniform and return current? But minimize may break.
       p = np.full(n, 1.0/n)
       # To avoid crashing, compute grad zero?
    else:
       p = e / s
    # maybe ensure finite; if any nan, reset
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
       p = np.full(n,1.0/n)
    A = fft(p, fsize)
    # q = real(ifft(A*A))
    temp = ifft(A * A)
    q = temp.real[:2*n-1]
    c = S2 * q
    # Clamp tiny negative? For LSE, negative could lower max; but due roundoff maybe -1e-15. We can do c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) to be safe. However gradient of clamped objective wrong; but negative tiny, ignore. Don't clamp for gradient? z=beta(c-cm), if negative small no effect. max may be slightly low if true max negative? no true positive.
    cm = c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(cm): ...
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    # To avoid all z very negative? cm max => at least one 0.
    ez = np.exp(z)
    tot = ez.sum()
    if tot <= 0 or not finite: w=np.full(2*n-1,1/(2*n-1)); val=cm?
    else: w=ez/tot; val=cm+np.log(tot)/beta
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    corr = ifft(W * np.conj(A)).real[:n]
    grad_p = 2.0 * S2 * corr
    dot = np.dot(p, grad_p)
    grad_u = p * (grad_p - dot)
    # Optional: if grad_u not finite, set zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
       grad_u = np.zeros(n)
    # update best exact if cm finite
    state['count'] +=1
    if cm < state['best_val'] - 1e-15:
       state['best_val']=float(cm); state['best_p']=p.copy()
    scale=state['scale']
    return scale*val, scale*grad_u
```

For n=8192, effect ~1e-6*log n ~tiny. Gradient wrt p: -1e-6*(log p +1 - dot). Could help keep p positive. But may bias by ~1e-6, negligible vs target 0.004. We can add tiny `reg=1e-8` maybe. Can we compute exact subgradient for β=inf? We can in final polish use exact top uniform with projected gradient to remove LSE bias. Let's implement a small exact polishing step after L-BFGS:
```
def exact_polish(p, n, max_time, rng):
    p=normalize_sum(p,1.0)
    S2=2*n; fsize=...
    lr=0.01
    best=p.copy(); best_c=inf
    for step in range while time:
       c,cm,A = ...
       if cm<best_c: best=p.copy(); best_c=cm
       # active lags within tol: tol = max(1e-6, 1e-5*(cm))
       active = c >= cm - 1e-5*max(1,cm)
       if active.sum()==0: active[argmax]=1
       w = zeros; w[active]=1/active.sum()
       corr=ifft(fft(w,fsize)*conj(A)).real[:n]
       g=2*S2*corr
       # project p - alpha*g for alpha in [lr, lr*0.5,...]
       trial_alpha=lr
       improved=False
       for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
           p_test = project_simplex(p - factor*lr*g, 1.0)
           c_test = fft_convolve_self(p_test, fsize); cm_test=S2*c_test.max()
           if cm_test < best_c* (1 - 1e-12) or cm_test < cm:
                p=p_test; best...; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           lr *=0.8
           if lr<1e-8: break
       # periodic noise
       if step%50==0: p=project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0,1e-5,n),1)
    return best,best_c
```
But each FFT test loops factors (5) and step; could be slow. Limit max_time 60s. Since L-BFGS likely enough, make this optional if time >200. It can improve exact max by moving along top subgradient.

Need `project_simplex` top-level robust:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   n=len(v); w=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cum=np.cumsum(w); idx=np.arange(1,n+1); tc=(cum-S)/idx; valid=tc<=w; if not any: theta=(v.sum()-S)/n else theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho; return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Works for S=1. Add small negative clamp.

Let's write `build_state`:
```
def build_state(n, beta, scale=1.0):
   m=3*n-2
   fsize=1
   while fsize < m: fsize <<=1
   S2=2.0*n
   return {'n':int(n),'fsize':fsize,'S2':S2,'beta':float(beta),'scale':float(scale),'count':0,'best_val':np.inf,'best_p':None}
```
For exact polish need fsize,S2; we can pass.

`objective`:
```
def objective(x):
   st=GLOBAL
   n=st['n']; fsize=st['fsize']; S2=st['S2']; beta=st['beta']; scale=st['scale']
   u=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if u.size != n: # pad/trunc?
      ...
   u = u - np.mean(u)
   umax = np.max(u)
   # If umax not finite, reset
   if not np.isfinite(umax):
       p=np.full(n,1.0/n)
   else:
       e=np.exp(u-umax)
       s=e.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
          p=np.full(n,1.0/n)
       else:
          p=e/s
   A=fft(p, fsize)
   c = S2 * ifft(A*A).real[:2*n-1]
   # If c has nan? replace
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       return scale*1e6, scale*np.zeros(n) # but may break; better fallback
   cm = float(np.max(c))
   z = beta * (c - cm)
   # Numerical: if beta very large and z all -inf except max 0? exp(0)=1, okay. If z has nan due inf? c finite. no.
   ez = np.exp(z)
   tot = float(np.sum(ez))
   if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0.0:
       val=cm
       w=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1))
   else:
       w=ez/tot
       val=cm + float(np.log(tot))/beta
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
       w=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1)); val=cm
   W=fft(w, fsize)
   corr=ifft(W*np.conj(A)).real[:n]
   grad_p=2.0*S2*corr
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)): grad_p=np.zeros(n)
   dot=float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
   grad_u=p*(grad_p-dot)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)): grad_u=np.zeros(n)
   st['count'] = int(st.get('count',0)) + 1
   if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < st['best_val'] - 1e-14:
       st['best_val']=cm; st['best_p']=p.copy()
   return scale*val, scale*grad_u
```
But `st` dict may not have best_p if updated in objective after state built. Good.

But true c nonnegative; roundoff -1e-16. Could do `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)` for value, but gradient weights should correspond to c; if we set negative to 0, tiny. Do `c_clean = np.maximum(c,0.0); cm=max; z=beta*(c_clean-cm)`. For gradient, weights on cleaned; fine. Ensure q positive. Use `c = S2 * np.real(...)`, then `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`.

Now `p_from_u`:
```
def p_from_u(u,n):
   u=np.asarray(u).reshape(-1)
   u=u-np.mean(u)
   e=np.exp(u-u.max())
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(n,1/n)
   return e/s
```

`init_u(p,n)`:
```
p=np.asarray(p).reshape(-1); p=np.maximum(p,0.0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=np.ones(n); else p=p/s
floor=1e-15
p=np.maximum(p, floor)
p=p/p.sum()
u=np.log(p)
u=u-np.mean(u)
return u
```

`resample`:
```
def resample(a, new_n):
   a=np.asarray(a).reshape(-1)
   if a.size==new_n: return a.copy()
   old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   return np.interp(new,old,a)
```

def run_lbzfs_stage(u0,n,beta,maxiter,maxfun,ftol=1e-10,gtol=1e-12):
   state=build_state(n,beta,1e6); GLOBAL.update(state)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   try:
      res=minimize(objective,u0,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'disp':False,'ftol':ftol,'gtol':gtol})
   except Exception as e:
      res=None
   elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
   # convert res.x to p if available
   p_res = p_from_u(res.x, n) if res is not None else None
   state_best = state['best_p']
   # choose better exact among p_res and state_best
   ...
   return p_best_stage, count, elapsed
```

```
def exact_polish(p, n, fsize, S2, max_time, rng):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).reshape(-1)
    p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n, inf
    p=p/s
    best=p.copy(); best_c=exact_value_p(p,...)
    lr=0.05
    tol=None
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    step=0
    nfull=2*n-1
    full_w=np.zeros(nfull)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step +=1
        A=fft(p,fsize)
        c=S2*np.maximum(ifft(A*A).real[:nfull],0.0)
        cm=c.max()
        if cm < best_c: best=p.copy(); best_c=cm
        if step%100==0: print('polish',step,best_c)
        # top k weights
        k = max(1, min(200, nfull//500))  # ~32 for 16383, 200 cap
        # get top k indices: argpartition
        if nfull > 2000:
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]  # ascending? returns k largest? need sort
        else: idx=np.argsort(c)[-k:]
        # But if ties at max, uniform.
        # To include all equal max within float: maybe active=c >= c[idx[0]]-1e-12; but k enough.
        w=np.zeros(nfull); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
        W=fft(w,fsize)
        corr=ifft(W*np.conj(A)).real[:n]
        g=2.0*S2*corr
        if not finite: break
        improved=False
        for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
            p_test = project_simplex(p - factor*lr*g, 1.0)
            c_test = S2*np.maximum(ifft(fft(p_test,fsize)**2).real[:nfull],0.0)
            cm_test=c_test.max()
            if cm_test < best_c * (1 - 1e-12) or cm_test < cm - 1e-12:
                p=p_test; improved=True; break
        if improved:
            if step % 10 ==0: lr *=1.1
        else:
            lr *=0.7
            if lr < 1e-8: break
            # optional noise
            noise = rng.normal(0, 1e-5, n)
            p = project_simplex(p + noise, 1.0)
    return best,best_c
```

Let's write `run_lbgfs_stage` carefully:
```
def run_lbgfs_stage(u0, n, beta, maxiter, maxfun, ftol=1e-10, gtol=1e-12, scale=1e6):
    global GLOBAL
    state = build_state(n, beta, scale)
    GLOBAL = state
    t0=time.monotonic()
    res=None
    try:
        res = minimize(objective, u0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxfun),
                                'disp': False, 'ftol': float(ftol), 'gtol': float(gtol), 'maxls': 20})
    except Exception:
        res=None
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
    count=max(1,int(GLOBAL.get('count',0)))
    p_res=None
    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
        p_res = p_from_u(res.x, n)
    p_state = GLOBAL.get('best_p')
    # choose best exact among p_res, p_state
    c_res = exact_value_p(p_res,n,state['fsize'],state['S2']) if p_res is not None else inf
    c_state = GLOBAL.get('best_val', np.inf)
    if p_state is not None and c_state < c_res:
        return p_state, count, elapsed
    elif p_res is not None and c_res < c_state:
        return p_res, count, elapsed
    elif p_state is not None:
        return p_state, count, elapsed
    else:
        return None, count, elapsed
```

def exact_value_p(p, n, fsize, S2):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size != n: ...
    A=fft(p, fsize)
    c=S2*np.maximum(ifft(A*A).real[:2*n-1],0.0)
    val=float(c.max())
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf
```

`candidate_list_from_p`:
```
def candidate_list_from_p(p, n):
    p=np.asarray(p).reshape(-1)
    if p.size != n: n=p.size
    if n<=0: return []
    p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-15 or not finite: p=np.ones(n)/n; s=p.sum()
    else: p=p/s
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=p*S
    # reduce if any >1000 (S can be up to sqrt(2*16384)=181, no)
    maxa=np.max(a)
    if maxa > 1000.0:
       a = a * (1000.0 / maxa)
    if np.sum(a) < 0.01:
       a = a + (0.01 - np.sum(a))/n
    # ensure finite
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```

In main loop, after stage:
```

def prepare_initial(seed, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    given=None
    for name in ('height_sequence_1','hight_sequence_1'):
       given = globals().get(name)
       if given is not None: break
    arr=None
    if given is not None:

       except: arr=None
    n=8192
    p=None

```
def exact_polish(p,n,fsize,S2,max_time,rng):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).reshape(-1); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n, exact_value_p(...)
   p=p/s
   best=p.copy(); best_c=exact_value_p(p,n,f-size,S2)
   full=2*n-1
   lr=0.1
   deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
   k=max(5, min(200, full//200))
   step=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      step+=1
      A=fft(p,fsize)
      c=S2*np.maximum(ifft(A*A).real[:full],0.0)
      cm=float(c.max())
      if cm < best_c:
         best_c=cm; best=p.copy()
      if cm <=0: break
      if k == 1: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
      else: idx=np.argpartition(c, full-k)[full-k:]  # returns k unsorted largest
      w=np.zeros(full); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
      W=fft(w,fsize)
      corr=ifft(W*np.conj(A)).real[:n]
      g=2.0*S2*corr
      if not finite: break
      gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
      if gmax <= 1e-300: break
      improved=False
      for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
          mult=np.exp(-factor*lr*g/gmax)
          p_test=p*mult
          st=p_test.sum()
          if st <=0 or not finite: continue
          p_test=p_test/st
          c_test=S2*np.maximum(ifft(fft(p_test,fsize)*??).real[:full],0.0)
```

```

In `run_lbgfs_stage`, after `GLOBAL = state`, if minimize fails before calling objective, count=0. But objective is called on initial x before? Usually yes. If not, count=1? We set count=max(1,int(GLOBAL.get('count',0))). Good.

Let's produce final code.
</think>

```python
"""
 softmax-LBFGS + exact peak-polish optimizer for
 min 2*n*max(self-convolution) / (sum)^2 over non-negative sequences.

 The sequence is optimized in the probability simplex p_i = a_i / sum(a),
 with sum(p)=1.  Then the objective is 2*n*max(p*p) (equivalently the usual
 normalized-convolution peak).  Optimizing in log-softmax coordinates keeps
 p_i > 0 and makes the problem smooth.  A softmax LSE approximation of the
 peak is used for gradient evaluation; beta is annealed.  A final multiplicative
 top-lag polish directly reduces the exact maximum.
"""
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore")

GLOBAL = {}


def next_power2_at_least(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def build_state(n, beta, scale=1.0, reg=1e-8):
    n = int(n)
    fsize = next_power2_at_least(3 * n - 2)
    S2 = 2.0 * float(n)
    return {
        "n": n,
        "fsize": fsize,
        "S2": S2,
        "beta": float(beta),
        "scale": float(scale),
        "reg": float(reg),
        "count": 0,
        "best_val": np.inf,
        "best_p": None,
    }


def p_from_u(u, n):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if u.size != n:
        if u.size > n:
            u = u[:n]
        else:
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v[:u.size] = u
            u = v
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    umax = float(np.max(u))
    if not np.isfinite(umax):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp(u - umax)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = e / s
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def init_u_from_p(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size != n:
        if p.size > n:
            p = p[:n]
        else:
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v[:p.size] = p
            p = v
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = n
    p = p / s
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-30)
    p = p / float(np.sum(p))
    u = np.log(p)
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    return u


def exact_value_p(p, n, fsize, S2):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size != n:
        if p.size > n:
            p = p[:n]
        else:
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v[:p.size] = p
            p = v
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    A = fft(p, fsize)
    c = S2 * np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1], 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return np.inf
    return float(np.max(c))


def objective(x):
    st = GLOBAL
    n = st["n"]
    fsize = st["fsize"]
    S2 = st["S2"]
    beta = st["beta"]
    scale = st["scale"]
    reg = st.get("reg", 0.0)

    p = p_from_u(x, n)
    A = fft(p, fsize)
    c = S2 * np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1], 0.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        st["count"] = int(st.get("count", 0)) + 1
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    tot = float(np.sum(ez))

    mfull = 2 * n - 1
    if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(mfull, 1.0 / mfull, dtype=np.float64)
        val = cm
    else:
        w = ez / tot
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
            w = np.full(mfull, 1.0 / mfull, dtype=np.float64)
            val = cm
        else:
            val = cm + float(np.log(tot)) / beta

    if reg > 0.0:
        ps = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        val = val + reg * float(np.sum(p * np.log(ps)))

    W = fft(w, fsize)
    corr = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    grad_p = 2.0 * S2 * corr

    if reg > 0.0:
        ps = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        grad_p = grad_p + reg * (np.log(ps) + 1.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
        grad_p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    dot = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_u = p * (grad_p - dot)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
        grad_u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    st["count"] = int(st.get("count", 0)) + 1
    if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < float(st.get("best_val", np.inf)) - 1e-14:
        st["best_val"] = cm
        st["best_p"] = p.copy()

    return scale * val, scale * grad_u


def run_lbgfs_stage(u0, n, beta, maxiter, maxfun, scale=1e6, reg=1e-8):
    global GLOBAL
    GLOBAL = build_state(n, beta=beta, scale=scale, reg=reg)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    res = None
    try:
        opts = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "disp": False,
            "ftol": 1e-10,
            "gtol": 1e-12,
            "maxls": 20,
        }
        res = minimize(objective, u0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=opts)
    except Exception:
        res = None
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    count = max(1, int(GLOBAL.get("count", 0)))

    p_res = None
    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
        p_res = p_from_u(res.x, n)

    p_best = GLOBAL.get("best_p")
    v_best = float(GLOBAL.get("best_val", np.inf))
    v_res = np.inf
    if p_res is not None:
        v_res = exact_value_p(p_res, n, GLOBAL["fsize"], GLOBAL["S2"])

    if p_res is not None and v_res < v_best:
        p_best = p_res
        v_best = v_res

    if p_best is None and p_res is not None:
        p_best = p_res
    if p_best is None:
        p_best = p_from_u(u0, n)

    return p_best, count, elapsed


def exact_polish(p, n, fsize, S2, max_time, rng):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = exact_value_p(p, n, fsize, S2)
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        return best_p, np.inf

    full = 2 * n - 1
    k = max(5, min(220, full // 180))
    lr = 0.08
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(max_time))
    step = 0
    stale = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step += 1
        A = fft(p, fsize)
        c = S2 * np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A * A))[:full], 0.0)
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_c:
            best_c = cm
            best_p = p.copy()
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1

        if cm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
            break

        if k <= 1:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, full - k)[full - k:]

        w = np.zeros(full, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(len(idx))
        W = fft(w, fsize)
        corr = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
        g = 2.0 * S2 * corr
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        improved = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            mult = np.exp(-factor * lr * g / gmax)
            p_test = p * mult
            stp = float(np.sum(p_test))
            if not np.isfinite(stp) or stp <= 0.0:
                continue
            p_test = p_test / stp
            A_test = fft(p_test, fsize)
            c_test = S2 * np.maximum(np.real(ifft(A_test * A_test))[:full], 0.0)
            cm_test = float(np.max(c_test))
            if np.isfinite(cm_test) and cm_test < best_c - 1e-12:
                p = p_test
                best_c = cm_test
                best_p = p.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.12)
            stale = 0
        else:
            lr *= 0.78
            if stale > 40:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n)
                mult = np.exp(noise)
                p_test = p * mult
                stp = float(np.sum(p_test))
                if np.isfinite(stp) and stp > 0.0:
                    p = p_test / stp
                stale = 0
            if lr <= 1e-5:
                break

    return best_p, best_c


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if a.size == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old_t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_t, old_t, a)
    return out


def prepare_initial(seed=42, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    given = None
    for name in ("height_sequence_1", "hight_sequence_1"):
        g = globals().get(name, None)
        if g is not None:
            given = g
            break

    arr = None
    if given is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        except Exception:
            arr = None

    default_n = 8192
    n = default_n
    p = None

    if arr is not None and arr.size > 0:
        a = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sa = float(np.sum(a))
        if np.isfinite(sa) and sa > 1e-15:
            L = a.size
            if 2048 <= L <= 12288:
                n = L
                p = a / sa
            elif L > 12288:
                n = default_n
                p = resample_sequence(a, n)
            elif L >= 16:
                n = default_n
                p = resample_sequence(a, n)
            else:
                n = default_n
                p = resample_sequence(a, n)

    if p is None:
        n = default_n
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        y = (
            1.0
            + 0.80 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (t - 0.18))
            + 0.35 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * (t - 0.33))
            + 0.25 * np.exp(-6.0 * (t - 0.72) ** 2)
        )
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.05)
        y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
        p = np.maximum(y, 1e-6)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = p / s

    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = n
    p = p / s
    return int(n), p, (arr if arr is not None else None)


def candidate_list_from_p(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size != n:
        n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return []

    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    p = p / s

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = p * S
    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if np.isfinite(amax) and amax > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / amax)

    asum = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(asum) or asum < 0.01:
        a = a + max(0.0, (0.01 - asum)) / float(n)

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def safe_score(lst):
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        v = float(v)
        if np.isfinite(v):
            return v
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start_all = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_all + max(60.0, budget_s - 120.0)

    n, p0, given_arr = prepare_initial(seed=seed, rng=rng)
    S2 = 2.0 * float(n)
    fsize = next_power2_at_least(3 * n - 2)

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_c = exact_value_p(best_p, n, fsize, S2)
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_c = exact_value_p(best_p, n, fsize, S2)

    pool = [(best_c, best_p.copy())]
    print("[init] n=%d best=%.9f" % (n, best_c), flush=True)

    u0 = init_u_from_p(best_p, n)
    cost = 0.035

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        p_stage, cnt, elapsed = run_lbgfs_stage(
            u0, n, beta=250.0, maxiter=200, maxfun=700, scale=1e6, reg=1e-8
        )
        if cnt > 0:
            c_est = elapsed / float(cnt)
            if np.isfinite(c_est) and c_est > 1e-5:
                cost = c_est
        cs = exact_value_p(p_stage, n, fsize, S2) if p_stage is not None else np.inf
        if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c:
            best_c = cs
            best_p = p_stage.copy()
            pool.append((best_c, best_p.copy()))
        u0 = init_u_from_p(best_p, n)
        print("[calib] cost=%.5fs/eval best=%.9f" % (cost, best_c), flush=True)

    stage_defs = [
        (1000.0, 400, 6000),
        (4000.0, 400, 5000),
        (12000.0, 300, 3500),
        (30000.0, 200, 2500),
    ]

    for beta, maxiter, pref_maxfun in stage_defs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 240:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now - 240
        if remaining <= 0:
            break
        cap = int(max(200, (remaining * 0.14) / max(cost, 1e-4)))
        maxfun = int(min(pref_maxfun, max(200, cap)))
        if maxfun < 200:
            maxfun = 200

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        p_stage, cnt, elapsed = run_lbgfs_stage(
            u0, n, beta=beta, maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxfun, scale=1e6, reg=1e-8
        )
        if cnt > 0:
            ce = elapsed / float(cnt)
            if np.isfinite(ce) and ce > 1e-5:
                cost = max(cost, ce)

        cs = exact_value_p(p_stage, n, fsize, S2) if p_stage is not None else np.inf
        if np.isfinite(cs):
            improved = cs < best_c - 1e-12
            if improved:
                best_c = cs
                best_p = p_stage.copy()
                pool.append((best_c, best_p.copy()))
                u0 = init_u_from_p(best_p, n)
                print(
                    "[lbgfs beta=%.0f] improved best=%.9f evals=%d"
                    % (beta, best_c, cnt),
                    flush=True,
                )
            else:
                u0 = init_u_from_p(best_p, n)
                u0 = u0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.015, n)
                u0 -= np.mean(u0)
                print(
                    "[lbgfs beta=%.0f] no exact improvement best=%.9f evals=%d"
                    % (beta, best_c, cnt),
                    flush=True,
                )
        else:
            print("[lbgfs beta=%.0f] no value evals=%d" % (beta, cnt), flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 120:
        alloc = min(450.0, max(30.0, (deadline - now - 120) * 0.32))
        p_pol, c_pol = exact_polish(best_p, n, fsize, S2, max_time=alloc, rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(c_pol) and c_pol < best_c:
            best_c = c_pol
            best_p = p_pol.copy()
            pool.append((best_c, best_p.copy()))
        print("[polish] best=%.9f" % best_c, flush=True)

    candidates = []

    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(candidate_list_from_p(best_p, n))

    for cval, pp in pool:
        if pp is None:
            continue
        cur = candidate_list_from_p(pp, len(pp))
        skip = False
        for e in candidates:
            if len(e) == len(cur) and np.max(np.abs(np.asarray(e) - np.asarray(cur))) < 1e-12:
                skip = True
                break
        if not skip:
            candidates.append(cur)
        if len(candidates) >= 4:
            break

    if given_arr is not None and 0 < given_arr.size <= 20000:
        g = np.clip(np.asarray(given_arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), 0.0, 1000.0)
        candidates.append([float(v) for v in g])

    candidates.append([1.0] * 2048)

    final_score = np.inf
    final_list = None

    for lst in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20:
            break
        if not isinstance(lst, list) or not lst:
            continue
        sc = safe_score(lst)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = sc
            final_list = lst
            print("[final candidate] score=%.9f len=%d" % (sc, len(lst)), flush=True)

    if final_list is None or final_score == np.inf:
        if best_p is not None:
            final_list = candidate_list_from_p(best_p, n)
            final_score = np.inf
        else:
            final_list = [1.0] * 1024

    arr = np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0 or float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(v) for v in arr]
```
